The Dialog docs page's canonical example is `render={<Button variant="ghost">Close</Button>}` with a text label, and a second example shows a glyph the reader supplies alongside `aria-label`. An X in Ultima's set would exist only to be rendered into a slot Ultima does not style. `AlertDialog.Close` is the same wrapper trigger, and the canonical Alert Dialog demo uses labelled Buttons for Cancel and Confirm rather than an X. `Toast.Close` and `Toast.Action` are the same shape: `render={<Button variant="ghost" size="sm" />}`.

**Icons in the docs.** `apps/docs` takes `@phosphor-icons/react` as a devDependency, for demos that need a glyph Ultima does not ship: a leading icon on a Button, icons on menu items, Dialog's close. Weight is set once to `regular` through `IconContext` rather than per icon. The printed source shows the import rather than hiding it, and each component page carries one line saying Ultima ships no icon dependency and any set works. None of this reaches the registry: the package is MIT, has no runtime dependencies, and is tree-shakeable with `sideEffects: false`, so only the icons a demo names reach the built site.

**Icon-only Button** stays the documented rule the accessibility contract (ULT-18) fixed. No `IconButton`, no `iconOnly` prop. The Button docs page prints the recipe: `aria-label`, the consumer's glyph as the only child, and a `style` override setting `paddingInline` to the block padding so the control is square at its size.

### Per-component notes

What a builder would otherwise guess, beyond the axes, the styled parts, and the accessibility contract.

- **Button.** The `accent` column of `variants[variant][tone]` is the behavior already decided: `solid` is the role fill with `-hover` and `-active` and `<role>-contrast` text; `outline` is a transparent fill with `--ult-color-border` and `--ult-color-text`; `ghost` is transparent with `--ult-color-text-muted`. The `danger` column recolors all three to the ruin role: `outline` takes `--ult-color-danger-border` and `--ult-color-danger-text`, `ghost` takes `--ult-color-danger-text` with a `--ult-color-danger-subtle` hover. Icon-only stays a documented rule, not a prop.
- **Badge.** One size: text step 3, `--ult-font-tracking-wide`, weight medium, `--ult-radius-full`, padding from space steps 2 and 4. The radius is a pill at any size, since a browser clamps `border-radius` to half the box's shorter side; what sets a badge's weight on the page is the padding, and steps 1 and 3 read as an annotation rather than a label. `subtle` is `<role>-subtle` fill, `<role>-text`, `<role>-border` hairline — mana's `.chip` exactly. `solid` is `<role>` fill, `<role>-contrast` text, transparent border. The `neutral` tone has no color role to resolve to, so it names neutral tokens directly: `subtle` takes `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-text-muted`, `--ult-color-border`; `solid` takes `--ult-color-surface-hover` and `--ult-color-text`, a pairing the contrast gate already covers. Badge is static and carries no interaction states.
- **Tabs.** `underline` gives `List` a bottom hairline and `Indicator` a 2px `--ult-color-accent` bar; the active `Tab` is `--ult-color-text`, the rest `--ult-color-text-muted`. `segmented` gives `List` a `--ult-color-surface-sunken` ground, `--ult-radius-md`, space step 1 of padding and gap, and `Indicator` becomes a `--ult-color-surface-raised` pill at `--ult-radius-sm` sitting behind the active tab. Both read `data-orientation`; both transition `Indicator` over `--ult-motion-fast`. `Panel` sets spacing only.
- **Meter.** `Track` is `--ult-color-surface-sunken` at `--ult-radius-full`, height space step 2. `Indicator` is the toned fill: `<role>` for the four hue tones, `--ult-color-border-strong` for `neutral`. `Value` is the toned number: `<role>-text`, or `--ult-color-text` for `neutral`. `tone` is set once on `Root` and reaches both through context, under Cross-part state; either part accepts its own `tone` to override, so a neutral number under a colored bar is still one extra prop. `meter.tsx` as first built took `tone` on each part and moved to this shape in the v0 build. `Indicator`'s width is the one place a dynamic style is allowed. `Label` is text step 2 uppercase with `--ult-font-tracking-wide` and `--ult-color-text-subtle`.
- **Dialog.** `Viewport` is styled and does the centering: `position: fixed`, `inset: 0`, `display: grid`, `place-items: center`, padding from space step 6, `z.popup`. `Popup` therefore sets no position and no transform of its own, which is why the shared `scale(0.98)` transition works on it at all — centering the popup with `translate(-50%, -50%)` would have fought it. `Popup` takes the overlay surface plus a `max-width` and `max-height: 100%` with `overflow: auto`. `Title` is text step 6, semibold, `--ult-font-leading-tight`; `Description` is text step 4 in `--ult-color-text-muted`. `modal` stays Base UI's default, and a Title is always rendered.
- **Dropdown Menu.** One shared item style is applied to `Item`, `LinkItem`, `CheckboxItem`, `RadioItem`, and `SubmenuTrigger`: `--ult-radius-md`, padding from space steps 2 and 4, text step 4, `data-highlighted` to `--ult-color-surface-hover`, `data-disabled` to reduced opacity, and no focus ring. `SubmenuTrigger` also highlights on `data-popup-open`. Submenus are in v0: the parts are on the namespace by the compound rule either way, so leaving `SubmenuTrigger` unstyled would ship a public part that renders broken. Checkbox and radio indicators occupy a fixed-width trailing slot. All labels align at the left. Popups use `--ult-radius-md`, space step 2 padding, and a minimum width of twice space step 12, including submenus. `Separator` is a `--ult-color-border` hairline with space step 2 of margin; `GroupLabel` uses text step 2 in `--ult-color-text-subtle`, preserving the caller’s casing and normal tracking.
- **Select.** `Trigger` is the control: the `size` axis heights, `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-border-strong`, `--ult-radius-md`, the focus ring, and `':is([aria-invalid="true"], [data-invalid])'` to `--ult-color-danger-border`, matching Input. `Value` reads `data-placeholder` for `--ult-color-text-subtle`. `Icon` is `--ult-color-text-subtle` and does not rotate — one less transition to keep in step with the popup's. Items share Menu's item style plus a `data-selected` weight change, and `ItemIndicator` is the same fixed leading slot. The scroll arrows are styled against the rule because they paint: a `--ult-color-surface-raised` band that hides the list edge, shown on `data-visible`. `alignItemWithTrigger` keeps Base UI's default; the popup style assumes no side, so `data-side="none"` needs no special case. The aligned popup injects an inline `<style>` to hide scrollbars, so the Select docs page keeps a one-line pointer to the CSP note, which now lives in Registry and install under What the consumer still does by hand.
- **Tooltip.** The narrow overlay: `--ult-radius-sm`, `--ult-shadow-sm`, text step 2, padding from space steps 2 and 3, and a `max-width` so long text wraps. `Arrow` is `--ult-color-surface-raised` with the same hairline. `Tooltip.Trigger` still requires `aria-label` in its types; that is unchanged by its passing through unstyled.
- **Switch.** One size, from the space scale: track space step 10 wide by step 8 tall at `--ult-radius-full`, thumb space step 7 at `--ult-radius-full` with space step 1 of inset. Track is `--ult-color-border-strong` unchecked and `--ult-color-accent` on `data-checked`; the thumb is `--ult-color-surface` plus the hairline border the forced-colors rule requires. The thumb transitions `translate` over `--ult-motion-fast`. The focus ring is on Root.
- **Input.** No notes beyond the `size` axis and the accessibility contract: `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-border-strong`, `--ult-radius-md`, the focus ring, `':is([aria-invalid="true"], [data-invalid])'` to `--ult-color-danger-border`, and `--ult-color-text-subtle` for `::placeholder`.
- **Textarea.** Settled on ULT-80. One file, `textarea.tsx`, Base UI `Input` with `render={<textarea />}` so Field registration is intact and Enter does not submit. Same paint as Input — `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-border-strong`, `--ult-radius-md`, the focus ring, the validation selector, `--ult-color-text-subtle` for `::placeholder` — except `size` sets `min-height` at space steps 9, 10, and 11 rather than `height`, `resize: vertical`, and a wrapping `line-height` rather than `--ult-font-leading-none`. Default `md`. Auto-resize is out of v0.1. `rows` and `cols` pass through. No Ultima `input` dependency: wrapping Input would inherit its `height` and put `input` in every textarea install.
- **Checkbox.** Settled on ULT-80. Three parts, no axes, two glyphs. `Root` is a space-step-6 square at `--ult-radius-sm`, `--ult-color-border-strong` unchecked, `--ult-color-accent` fill and `--ult-color-accent-contrast` color on `':is([data-checked], [data-indeterminate])'`, the focus ring, reduced opacity on `':is([data-disabled])'`, and the validation selector. `Indicator` is the glyph slot: the private check on `data-checked`, the private dash on `data-indeterminate`, `children` replacing the slot, and `':is([data-unchecked])': { display: 'none' }` so the exit-transition window does not flash. `Group` is a flex column with space step 3 of gap, wrapping Base UI's bare `CheckboxGroup`. `indeterminate` and `parent` pass through; Ultima does not set the native property. Registry item `checkbox`, one file, two Base UI subpaths, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Radio Group.** Settled on ULT-80. Three parts, no axes, the filled-circle glyph. `Root` is a flex column with space step 3 of gap. `Item` is a space-step-6 circle at `--ult-radius-full`, the same rest, checked, disabled, invalid, and ring treatment as `Checkbox.Root` minus `data-indeterminate`. `Indicator` is the private dot, the same `data-unchecked` rule, `children` replacing the slot. Registry item `radio-group`, one file, two Base UI subpaths, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Combobox.** Settled on ULT-81. Sibling of Select, not a variant, not Autocomplete. Twenty-six parts, `size` on `InputGroup`, default `md`. `useFilter`, `useFilteredItems`, and `createItems` sit on the namespace as values. `InputGroup` is the visual box: the three control heights, `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-border-strong`, `--ult-radius-md`, `':is([aria-invalid="true"], [data-invalid])'` to `--ult-color-danger-border`, and the ring via `:focus-within`. `Input` is borderless inside it, with `--ult-color-text-subtle` for `::placeholder` and `data-placeholder`. `Trigger` is the styled chevron button, not a wrapper trigger. `Icon` does not rotate. Items share Menu's item style plus a `data-selected` weight change, restated in this file rather than imported, and `ItemIndicator` is Select's leading slot. `Popup` takes the overlay surface, `z.popup`, the shared enter/exit, `width: var(--anchor-width)`, and `max-width: var(--available-width)`. `List` is the scroll container: `overflow-y: auto`, `overscroll-behavior: contain`, `max-height: min(<n>, var(--available-height))`, matching block padding and scroll-padding. `Empty` and `Status` stay mounted; they are `role="status"` and the empty state is this part, not the v0.1 Empty component. `Clear` shows on `data-visible`. Glyphs: chevron-down, check, x, `children` replace the slot. No filter library. Canonical demo is Input-as-control; a second shows the Label trap (`Combobox.Label` names Trigger, `nativeLabel={false}`); a third is multiple/chips. Grid is not a v0.1 documented example. `Combobox.InputGroup` is this component's part, not the v0.2 Input Group catalogue entry. `data-popup-open`, `data-highlighted`, `data-selected`, `data-disabled`, `data-placeholder`, and `data-visible` (on Clear) drive style. Registry item `combobox`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Slider.** Settled on ULT-81. Seven parts, no axes, no `tone`. `Root` passes through. `Label` is text step 3, weight medium, `--ult-color-text`. `Value` is text step 3, an `<output htmlFor>` with Base UI's `aria-live="off"` so dragging does not spam; `aria-valuetext` stays the locale-formatted default, overridable per thumb. `Control` carries `touch-action: none` and `user-select: none`; its padding and border are inputs to the pointer math. `Track` is `--ult-color-surface-sunken` at `--ult-radius-full` with space-step-2 cross-axis size, the same geometry as Meter's track without taking Meter's `tone`. `Indicator` is `--ult-color-accent`; position and length come inline. `Thumb` is a raised knob with the hairline the forced-colors rule already uses on Switch, and the ring is `:has(:focus-visible)`. Range is a second example: one `Thumb` per value, `index` per thumb for SSR, a distinct `aria-label` per thumb when there is no visible `Label`. `orientation`, `min`, `max`, `step`, `largeStep`, and `thumbAlignment` pass through. Canonical `thumbAlignment` is `"center"`, which injects no script; `"edge"` is the CSP note. `data-dragging`, `data-orientation`, and `data-disabled` drive style. No Ultima `input` dependency. Registry item `slider`, one file.
- **Alert.** Settled on ULT-82. Four parts, `tone` only, default `neutral`. `Root` is a `<div>` through `useRender`, a flex row with space step 4 of gap, padding from space step 5, `--ult-radius-md`, and Badge's `subtle` column: `<role>-subtle` fill, `<role>-text`, `<role>-border` hairline; `neutral` names `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-text-muted`, `--ult-color-border`. Title, Description, and Icon inherit the text color; Alert writes no context. `Title` is text step 5, semibold, `--ult-font-leading-tight`, an `<h3>` through `useRender`. `Description` is text step 4. `Icon` is omitted when unused: `1em`, `flex-shrink: 0`, `aria-hidden`. No built-in glyph. No live role. The docs' `Note` is not this component. Registry item `alert`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Alert Dialog.** Settled on ULT-82. Separate item from Dialog, not a recipe. Every Base UI part; styled split identical to Dialog. Restate Viewport, Backdrop, Popup, Title, and Description with Dialog's overlay tokens and paint: Viewport centers, Popup takes the overlay surface plus max-width and max-height, Title is text step 6 semibold tight leading, Description is text step 4 in `--ult-color-text-muted`. Do not import `dialog`. Trigger and Close are wrapper triggers. Title always rendered; Description always rendered. `modal` and `disablePointerDismissal` are not props. Escape closes; backdrop click does not. Canonical demo is Title, Description, Cancel then Confirm as Buttons, Cancel first in DOM so initial focus is not the destructive action. `Handle` / `createHandle` passes through as a value. Registry item `alert-dialog`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Toast.** Settled on ULT-83. Outside the overlay recipe. Every Base UI part; `useToastManager` and `createToastManager` sit on the namespace. `Provider` is a pass-through part: the consumer mounts `Provider` + `Portal` + `Viewport` once in their tree, and Portal and Viewport stay mounted when the stack is empty. Ultima does not ship an app shell, does not wrap `<html>`, and does not invent `useToast()`. One Provider is the v0.1 documented setup; a second Provider for anchored toasts is Base UI's and is not documented as required. Viewport is `position: fixed`, `inset-block-end` and `inset-inline-end` from space step 6, `z.toast`, and the `--toast-frontmost-height` clamp. Root restates the overlay surface tokens — `--ult-color-surface-raised`, hairline `--ult-color-border`, `--ult-radius-lg`, `--ult-shadow-md`, border and shadow together — plus Badge's `subtle` column on `[data-type]`: `<role>-subtle` fill, `<role>-text`, `<role>-border` hairline; `neutral` names `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-text-muted`, `--ult-color-border`. `error` paints as `danger`; `loading` paints as `neutral`. Do not import Badge or Alert. Stacking CSS is the full Base UI demo recipe: intra-stack `z-index`, `--toast-offset-y` translate, swipe translates, `data-swipe-direction` dismiss, height clamp. Durations read `--ult-motion-fast`, not a millisecond literal. The `1000` in `calc(1000 - var(--toast-index))`, the scale step, and the swipe-dismiss percentages are module constants in `toast.tsx`. Enter/exit is opacity plus those transforms, not overlay `scale(0.98)`. Content fades on `[data-behind]` and returns on `[data-expanded]`. Title is text step 4, semibold, `--ult-font-leading-tight`. Description is text step 3 in `--ult-color-text-muted`. Action and Close are wrapper triggers, canonical `render={<Button variant="ghost" size="sm" />}`. Positioner is `outline: 0`. Arrow is `--ult-color-surface-raised` with the same hairline, for the anchored mode that is on the namespace and not a v0.1 documented example. `limit`, `timeout`, `swipeDirection`, and `priority` pass through and are not axes. Colour never carries meaning alone. `data-type`, `data-expanded`, `data-limited`, `data-swiping`, `data-swipe-direction`, `data-behind`, `data-starting-style`, and `data-ending-style` drive style. Swipe is proof bar item 4; stacking CSS is item 8; item 7 is not this component. Registry item `toast`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Progress.** Settled on ULT-84. Five parts, Meter's `tone` on `Root`, default `neutral`, reaching Indicator and Value through file-private context, each overridable. Do not import Meter. Restate Track geometry: `--ult-color-surface-sunken` at `--ult-radius-full`, height space step 2, `overflow: hidden`. Label is text step 2 uppercase with `--ult-font-tracking-wide` and `--ult-color-text-subtle`. Indicator is the toned fill: `<role>` for the four hue tones, `--ult-color-border-strong` for `neutral`. Value is the toned number: `<role>-text`, or `--ult-color-text` for `neutral`. Determinate width is Base UI's inline `%`; `transitionProperty: width` over `--ult-motion-base` with `standard` easing. Indeterminate is `[data-indeterminate]` on Indicator: this file's keyframes, duration `--ult-motion-loop`, and under reduced motion a static 25% width with `animationName: none`. `value={null}` is Base UI's; there is no `variant="indeterminate"`. Root is a flex column with space step 2 of gap. `data-complete`, `data-indeterminate`, and `data-progressing` drive style; exactly one is present. Determinate width transition and indeterminate `animation-name` are proof bar item 8. Item 7 is not this component. Registry item `progress`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Skeleton.** Settled on ULT-84. One part, `Root`, no axes. `--ult-color-surface-sunken` at `--ult-radius-md`. Pulse keyframes in this file, duration `--ult-motion-loop`; under reduced motion, rest opacity and `animationName: none`. Size is the `style` slot. `aria-hidden="true"`. `animation-name` is proof bar item 8; `aria-hidden` is item 2. Item 7 is not this component. Registry item `skeleton`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Spinner.** Settled on ULT-84. One part, `Root`, no axes, no `tone`. A circular looping mark in CSS, hairline border, `currentColor`. Keyframes in this file, duration `--ult-motion-loop`; under reduced motion, a static glyph and `animationName: none`. `aria-hidden="true"`. Not a Progress and not a toast. `animation-name` is proof bar item 8; `aria-hidden` is item 2. Item 7 is not this component. Registry item `spinner`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Empty.** Settled on ULT-84. Four parts, no axes. `Root` is a `<div>` through `useRender`, a centered flex column with space step 4 of gap, padding from space step 8, `--ult-radius-md`, and `--ult-color-surface-sunken`. `Title` is text step 5, semibold, `--ult-font-leading-tight`, an `<h3>` through `useRender`. `Description` is text step 4 in `--ult-color-text-muted`. `Icon` is omitted when unused: `1em`, `flex-shrink: 0`, `aria-hidden`. No built-in glyph. An action is children, a catalogue Button. No live role. Not Card, not Alert, not `Combobox.Empty`. Registry item `empty`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Command.** Settled on [#280](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/280) over the spine on [#277](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/277). A catalogue item on Base UI `Autocomplete`, not a recipe over `combobox`, because the free-text and restricted branches put different props live under the second clause. Twenty-one parts, `size` on `InputGroup`, default `md`. `useFilter` and `useFilteredItems` sit on the namespace as values; the primitive exports no `createItems`, so the namespace carries two where Combobox's carries three. Styled: `InputGroup` (the visual box: the three control heights, `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `--ult-color-border-strong`, `--ult-radius-md`, `':is([aria-invalid="true"], [data-invalid])'` to `--ult-color-danger-border`, and the ring via `:focus-within`), `Input` (borderless inside it, `--ult-color-text-subtle` for `::placeholder`), `Trigger` (the styled chevron button, not a wrapper trigger), `Icon` (`--ult-color-text-subtle`, does not rotate), `Clear` (x glyph, shows on `data-visible`), `Popup` (the overlay surface, `z.popup`, the shared enter/exit, `width: var(--anchor-width)`, `max-width: var(--available-width)`), `Arrow`, `List` (the scroll container in the anchored arrangement: `overflow-y: auto`, `overscroll-behavior: contain`, `max-height: min(<n>, var(--available-height))`, matching block padding and scroll-padding), `Item` (Menu's item style minus the indicator slot and the `data-selected` weight — autocomplete items are actions and suggestions, not a selection — `data-highlighted` to `--ult-color-surface-hover`), `Status` and `Empty` (`role="status"`, always mounted; the empty state is this part, not the `empty` item), `Separator` (a `--ult-color-border` hairline with space step 2 of margin), and `GroupLabel` (text step 2 in `--ult-color-text-subtle`). Passed through: `Root`, `Value` (renders no element), `Collection`, `Portal`, `Backdrop` (an invisible click-catcher), `Positioner` (`outline: 0`), `Group`, `Row`. Do not import `combobox` or `input`: restate the item style, the `InputGroup` box, the borderless `Input`, and the chevron-down and x glyphs in `command.tsx`, the same reason Combobox does not import Select. `filter` is the scorer socket: the default is the primitive's Collator filter, `command.tsx` imports no filter library because the engine is the consumer's dependency under ADR 0007, and the command-dialog recipe documents the optional `match-sorter` wiring. `mode`, `autoHighlight`, `keepHighlight`, `highlightItemOnHover`, `openOnInputClick`, `submitOnItemClick`, `itemToStringValue`, `items`, `filteredItems`, and `value` / `defaultValue` / `onValueChange` pass through and are not axes. The anchored `Popup` is the thirteenth overlay and joins the recipe unvaried; `inline` renders no popup. Item 7 is not this component; item 8 covers `List` as scroll container and `Popup` tracking `--anchor-width`, Combobox's named successors. Glyphs: chevron-down on `Icon`, x on `Clear`, `children` replace the slot. `data-popup-open`, `data-highlighted`, `data-visible` (on Clear), and the Field-root state on `InputGroup` drive style. Registry item `command`, one file, one new subpath `@base-ui/react/autocomplete` in both vitest `optimizeDeps.include` lists, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Command dialog.** The searchable command-dialog example is the entry's recipe, living as a `## Command dialog` section on Command's page, settled on [#280](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/280). Dependencies `dialog`, `command`, `scroll-area`, `button`; engine an optional `match-sorter` scorer the consumer installs and wires through `Command.Root`'s `filter` prop (`fuzzysort` the alternative); checks the live demo in the axe sweep and the documented states on the page. Dialog owns the surface whole and `dialog.tsx` does not change: `Dialog.Trigger render={<Button />}` opens it, and the recipe top-anchors `Dialog.Viewport` through the `style` slot — `items-start` plus top padding, where Base UI's own command-palette example anchors top rather than centering. Inside the Popup, `Command.Root open inline items={...} autoHighlight="always" keepHighlight` holds `InputGroup` and `Input`; `ScrollArea.Root` / `Viewport` / `Content` wraps `Empty` and `List` with its `Group`, `GroupLabel`, `Collection`, and `Item`, and `ScrollArea.Viewport` is the bounded, keyboard-reachable scroll region. `Dialog.Title` renders clip-hidden — the live-region clip pattern — because Title is always rendered, and `Dialog.Close` renders as a Button, hidden until focused, the in-loop close Base UI's example ships as an sr-only button. The keyboard contract: focus opens on `Input` (the first focusable element, no `initialFocus`); arrows move the highlight and `autoHighlight="always"` with `keepHighlight` guarantee Enter always has a target; Enter activates the highlighted item and the item's handler runs the action and closes the dialog; Escape closes the dialog, since `inline` mode has no autocomplete popup to consume it; Tab loops through `Input`, the hidden `Close`, and `ScrollArea.Viewport` when it overflows — items are reached by arrows, never Tab; focus returns to the trigger on close. The demo wires `Cmd/Ctrl+K` to open and keeps the shortcut-hint footer (`Activate` — `Enter`; `Close` — `Esc`) in platform `<kbd>` markup, the visible statement of the keyboard contract on the page. The recipe carries no engine install: the demo runs the default Collator filter, and the page documents the `filter`-prop scorer wiring as the optional step.
- **Field and Fieldset.** Settled on Field and Label: parts, and how validation state reaches a field's siblings (ULT-79); the parts, the styled split, and the absent axes are in The v0.1 set, the wiring is in the accessibility contract, and only the paint is here. `Field.Root` is a flex column with space step 2 of gap, which is the whole of its styling. `Label` is text step 3, weight medium, `--ult-color-text`. `Description` is text step 2 in `--ult-color-text-muted` with `margin: 0`, since it renders a `<p>` and the UA margin would double the root's gap. `Error` is text step 2 in `--ult-color-danger-text`. `Item` is a row with space step 3 of gap, for a checkbox or radio and its own label. `Fieldset.Root` resets the UA `<fieldset>`'s border, margin, and padding to zero and then lays out as a flex column with space step 3 of gap; `Legend` is text step 3, weight medium, and takes space step 2 of bottom padding. None of the five carries a hover, an active, or a focus state: a Field paints no control, and every interactive element inside one is a component that brings its own. Registry items `field` and `fieldset`, one file each, no Ultima component dependency in either — the control reaches a Field through the consumer's own markup, which is what keeps `field.tsx` free of the import that would otherwise reach every form component.
- **Sidebar.** The largest contract in v0, and the only one that spans three decisions: its parts and styling, its responsive state model, and its mobile menu. It is written out as its own subsection below this list.
- **Collapsible.** Decided on Contracts for the reusable patterns the docs pulls into v0 (ULT-57), priced on ULT-49. Three parts, no axes, no glyph slot. `Root` passes through. `Trigger` is a wrapper trigger and ships no styles at all, not even the ring: it is a real control carrying its own `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`, but `stylex.props` does not merge foreign class strings, and the spec already composes a nested Sidebar group as `Collapsible.Trigger render={<Sidebar.Button />}`. Canonical examples are `render={<Button variant="ghost" />}` standalone and `render={<Sidebar.Button />}` inside a sidebar; a caret is the consumer's glyph, rotating on `data-panel-open` (not `data-popup-open`). `Panel` is the only styled part and its animation is a hard contract: `height: var(--collapsible-panel-height)`, `overflow: hidden`, `transitionProperty: height`, `transitionDuration` from `--ult-motion-base`, and `height: 0` on both `':is([data-starting-style])'` and `':is([data-ending-style])'`. Base UI detects the animation by reading computed style, so a zero duration means the panel unmounts on the same frame with no animation and no warning; the motion tokens collapse to `1ms` under reduced motion, which is still non-zero, the same property the Overlays section already relies on. A keyframe animation beside the transition logs a dev warning and the transition wins, so the panel never carries one. `Panel` sets no `display`, so the UA `[hidden]` rule stands on its own, and it ships `':is([hidden]):not([hidden="until-found"])': { display: 'none' }` anyway, because the `style` slot lets a consumer add `display: grid` for their content and silently break find-in-page. `var()` passes through the StyleX compiler verbatim and the state keys land in the same layer at a higher priority than the default, both verified against this repo's `stylex.options.ts`, so no dynamic style is needed and the runtime-numbers-only rule is untouched. `--ult-motion-base` rather than `-fast` because a panel opening is the same gesture as Sidebar's desktop collapse. Registry item `collapsible`, one file, no Ultima component dependency: Button reaches it through `render` from the consumer.
- **Toggle Group.** Decided on Contracts for the reusable patterns the docs pulls into v0 (ULT-57). Two parts, `Root` and `Item`, one file importing both `@base-ui/react/toggle-group` and `@base-ui/react/toggle`. No axes, no glyph slot. Both parts are styled: the look is the segmented control, a sibling of Tabs' `segmented` variant rather than a copy of Button's tables. `Root` is a `--ult-color-surface-sunken` ground at `--ult-radius-md` with space step 1 of padding and gap; `Item` is a row at `--ult-radius-sm`, text step 3, `--ult-color-text-muted` resting, `--ult-color-text` on `':is([data-pressed])'` over `--ult-color-surface-raised`, plus hover, the focus ring, and reduced opacity on `':is([data-disabled])'`. Button is deliberately untouched: a `data-pressed` row would have to be written into every cell of the nested `variants[variant][tone]` lookup, five of whose six cells exist only to never use it. **That decision is reaffirmed on stronger ground now that Toggle is its own item**, on [#141](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/141): `variants` in `button.tsx` is module-private, so the row would be unreachable from `toggle.tsx` and would buy Toggle nothing, and Button renders `@base-ui/react/button`, a different component with no `pressed` prop and no `aria-pressed` to manage. The row would serve only a consumer hand-writing `aria-pressed` onto a Button, which **The line between a component and page layout** rules out. `toggle.tsx` restates this file's item table rather than importing it and rather than being imported by it, the way `context-menu.tsx` restates `dropdown-menu.tsx`: Toggle Group composes none of Toggle's behavior, that being `ToggleGroupContext`'s below Ultima, so **Restatement** allows no import; the two tables are meant to differ, since a standalone toggle needs its own resting surface where `Item` is transparent over a sunken ground, which is the Pagination-from-Button case rather than the Alert-Dialog-from-Dialog one; and an import would put `toggle` in this item's derived registry `dependencies`, so every Toggle Group install would pull Toggle. This file is unchanged by Toggle's arrival. `orientation`, `multiple`, `loopFocus`, `disabled`, and the `<Value extends string>` generic pass through as Base UI's props and are not axes. A single-selection group can be emptied — with `multiple: false` Base UI computes `nextPressed ? [newValue] : []`, so clicking the pressed item emits `[]` — and there is no `required` prop; a caller who needs a selection always present calls `eventDetails.cancel()` on the empty change, which Base UI honours before it sets state, and the docs theme control does exactly that. Registry item `toggle-group`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Card, Stat, Code.** Unchanged. They have no axes beyond Code's `variant`, no pass-through parts, and no state.
- **Table.** `Table.Scroll` is the one addition, decided on Contracts for the reusable patterns the docs pulls into v0 (ULT-57): an optional outer part, a `<div tabIndex={0} role="region">` with `overflow: auto` and the standard focus ring, naming itself from the caption through `aria-labelledby`. Optional because a table that fits needs no region and an empty one would add a stray landmark. It wraps `Table.Root` rather than replacing it, so `Root` stays one element and its `render` stays unambiguous, and a minimum width goes through the `style` slot on `Root` rather than becoming a prop. **Its `tabIndex` is conditional and its `role` is not**, corrected on [Whether Table.Scroll stays its own part now that Scroll Area exists, or the two converge](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/166) after [Scroll Area's contract](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/145) found the drift: `0` while `scrollWidth` exceeds `clientWidth`, an explicit `-1` when it does not, measured by a `ResizeObserver` over the part and its child in a module-private hook in `table.tsx`. Explicit `-1` rather than no attribute at all, because Chromium makes a native scroller focusable from 132 and an absent `tabindex` would hand the decision back to the engine, which is the sentence under the accessibility contract about both regions carrying an explicit one. This is **the second plain component with an effect**, after Sidebar's `useIsMobile`, and the hook stays module-private the way `styles.item` does in `toggle-group.tsx`. No overflow data attribute is exposed: nothing paints on one, and it would be public surface bought for nothing. The caption is documented as required under `Table.Scroll` rather than type-required, so the enforced total stays three. **This is the focus-ring copy running back the other way**: Scroll Area took `Table.Scroll`'s ring, and `Table.Scroll` takes `ScrollArea.Viewport`'s tab-order rule, each by restatement and neither by dependency. It is proof-bar item 7 for Table, behavior Ultima wires itself, and it does not uncheck line 519, which is a shipped part being corrected rather than an unshipped one. Everything else about Table in v0 is unchanged. Two parts join it in v0.2, for the Data Table recipe, under Data Table below; they are not v0 and the tables above do not list them.
- **Breadcrumb.** Settled on [Breadcrumb's parts, its separator, and the name on its landmark](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/88). Five parts, no axes, one glyph. Every part is plain and rendered through `useRender`, so each takes `render`. `Root` is the `<nav>` and a flex row. `List` is an `<ol>` reset to no margin, padding, or marker: flex row, `flex-wrap: wrap`, space step 2 of gap, text step 3. `Item` is an `<li>`, `display: inline-flex`, `align-items: center`. `Link` is an `<a>` at `--ult-color-text-muted`, `--ult-color-text` on `:hover`, `--ult-radius-sm`, and the focus ring; its `active` and the current style are the four-component mechanism under the accessibility contract. The last crumb stays a real `<a href>`, which is what the APG example and Primer render; shadcn/ui's and React Aria's `<span role="link" aria-disabled="true">` is not, because it announces "link" for something that does not navigate. A design wanting a non-navigable final crumb writes `render={<span />}`, which the APG permits, since `aria-current` is optional when the crumb is not a link. There is no `Page` part. `Separator` is an `<li role="presentation" aria-hidden="true">` holding the private chevron-right at `1em` with `flex-shrink: 0` and `--ult-color-text-subtle`, `children` replacing it; `role="presentation"` keeps it out of the list's item count and `aria-hidden` keeps its subtree out of the accessibility tree. There is no `Ellipsis` part: a trail too long for its container wraps, and a collapsed one is a `Breadcrumb.Item` holding a Dropdown Menu whose trigger is a `...` Button with an `aria-label` and whose items are the hidden crumbs, which is the shape Data Table's view options already take and adds no part and no dependency to the item. shadcn/ui's `BreadcrumbEllipsis` is not copied: its `sr-only` "More" sits inside the `aria-hidden="true"` span, where nothing announces it, and the collapsed crumbs leave the accessibility tree altogether. Two obligations are the consumer's and the docs page states them: a second breadcrumb on one page needs its own name, and the trail's last crumb is the current page. Registry item `breadcrumb`, one file, no Ultima component dependency and no Base UI subpath beyond `use-render`.
- **Pagination.** Settled on [Pagination's parts, its semantics, and whether Ultima owns the page window](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/89) and [Whether Pagination's controls come from Button, and what that means for a system with no className](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/91). Seven parts, no axes, one glyph, one exported pure function, and no context at all. **It is a catalogue component permanently rather than a recipe**: ADR 0007's test is a composition whose behavior needs an engine, and truncation is arithmetic with no package behind it, while the Data Table entry names `pagination` among its *component* dependencies, which a recipe has no installable unit to satisfy. `Pagination.getPages({ page, count, siblingCount = 1, boundaryCount = 1 })` returns `Array<{ type: 'page'; page: number } | { type: 'ellipsis' }>` at module scope, which is Zag and Ark's `api.pages` shape without the state machine. It is a value on the namespace and **not** `usePagination()`: it is pure arithmetic with no state and no effects, a component hook is defined as reading shared runtime state and throwing outside its Root, and the `use*` name would forbid calling it in a loop or a condition, which is where a caller wants it. MUI's `siblingCount` and `boundaryCount` names are kept because consumers know them. The caller owns the map from that list to the parts, because Cross-part state already ruled out a data prop that "cannot take the consumer's router link through `render`", and a page item is a router link in exactly that way. `Page`, `Previous`, and `Next` default to `<a>` and take `render={<button />}` for client-driven state. **`pagination.tsx` writes its own tables and declares no Ultima component dependency**, which is the restating rule under The registry item below; wrapper triggers were rejected because a part that ships no styles at all would leave Ultima shipping no pagination appearance, Button has no `[data-active]` row so `current` would drive nothing, and reaching an anchor would need two nested `render`s per item. One shared row style across the three, the way `Sidebar.Link` and `Sidebar.Button` share one: `height` and `minInlineSize` both `--ult-space-10` so an item is square until a third digit widens it, `paddingInline` space step 2 on `Page` and step 4 on the text-bearing arrows, text step 3, `--ult-radius-md`, `textDecoration: none`, `--ult-color-text-muted` resting, `--ult-color-text` over `--ult-color-surface-hover` on `:hover`, and the focus ring. Current is `--ult-color-text` at weight medium on `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, `Sidebar.Link`'s treatment minus its inline-start accent bar, which is a vertical-list affordance. Disabled is reduced opacity and `cursor: default`, and deliberately not `pointer-events: none`, which would also kill the hover state. That covers the three gaps the prior art found in Button — no `neutral` tone, no square size, no current-page row — in this file rather than by growing Button for one caller. `List` is a flex row with space step 1 of gap; `Ellipsis` takes the same box as an item so the row does not jitter as the window shifts, without the hover or the ring, and holds the private ellipsis glyph. **`pagination.tsx` mounts no live region**: Pagination sees pages and never items, so it cannot say "Showing 21 through 40 of 312", which is what Primer actually announces, and on the URL-driven default the page navigates and there is nothing to announce. That stays the Live regions pattern and the consumer's, which is already what this specification assigns to Data Table's row-count announcement. A caller must not render an Ultima Button into these parts, for the reason under Styled parts: two Ultima styles collide rather than cascade. Registry item `pagination`, one file, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Navigation Menu.** Settled on [Navigation Menu's parts, its two nav landmarks, and whether it is the eighth overlay](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/92), priced on ULT-49, measured on [#85](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/85), and admitted to the release on [#86](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/86). Thirteen parts, nine styled, no axes, one glyph. Its overlay variation is under Overlays above and its styled split has a row in the Styled parts table. **The morph reads four variables Base UI writes imperatively from the trigger**, first to `auto` and then to measured pixels: `--positioner-width` and `--positioner-height` on `Positioner`, `--popup-width` and `--popup-height` on `Popup`. If the stylesheet does not read them the menu does not animate between items at all, it snaps; a take-one-away run showed the Popup's pair carrying the morph and the Positioner's pinning the anchored box at the target size. `Positioner` also takes `max-width: var(--available-width)`, and `Viewport` takes `position: relative`, `overflow: hidden`, and 100% of both axes or the content is not clipped during the morph. Two hard constraints, both measured rather than read: **every cancelling rule is written as a longhand**, under State styling above, and **`data-instant`'s resize window is a fixed 100ms Base UI constant** that does not scale with the duration token, so `--ult-motion-base` fits under it and a longer duration would resume mid-resize. Reduced motion needs no extra rule and, unlike Collapsible, there is no minimum-duration contract: `navigation-menu/` never calls `getComputedStyle`, so a duration collapsed to `1ms` breaks nothing. A `stylex.create` value that *sets* a custom property compiles at priority 1, the earliest layer, so the maintainers' local `--duration` / `--easing` pattern is closed and the tokens are read at each use site instead. `Trigger` is styled and is not a wrapper trigger: it *is* the control, the same reasoning that keeps `Select.Trigger` and `Combobox.Trigger` styled. `Backdrop` is an invisible click-catcher and stays unstyled, like Menu's and Select's. `Link` takes Base UI's own `active`. `keepMounted` stays unset, as the recipe requires. The two landmarks and their names are in The navigation set. It portals, so it inherits the two global rules already documented for portalling components, `isolation: isolate` on the app root and `body { position: relative }` for iOS 26 Safari; `Root` mounts its own `FloatingTree` when it is not nested, so nesting needs no provider and the never-a-provider rule is untouched. One obstacle belongs to its build ticket rather than to this contract: `packages/ui/vitest.config.ts` needs `@base-ui/react/navigation-menu` in `optimizeDeps.include`, or the suite fails with a duplicate-React "Invalid hook call". Registry item `navigation-menu`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency.

- **Popover.** Settled on [Popover's parts, its styled split, and what names it](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/108). Eleven parts, five styled, no axes, no glyph. Its overlay row and styled split are in The overlay set above. `Viewport` carries `position: relative` and nothing else: the snapshot Base UI renders during a trigger change is absolutely positioned, and it rides the page flow from an unpositioned container. The `data-activation-direction` and transition attributes it emits stay unused, because Ultima morphs nothing there. `Title` and `Description` paint type; `Arrow` reuses `Tooltip.Arrow`'s geometry. `Backdrop` stays unstyled: `modal` keeps Base UI's `false` default, so the backdrop catches clicks and blocks nothing, and the popup keeps native scrolling on touch devices. `Trigger` and `Close` are wrapper triggers documented as `render={<Button />}`; `Root` and `Portal` group and portal. `Title` is optional and the popup name is the documented rule under the accessibility contract: `Title` when rendered, else `aria-label` or `aria-labelledby` on `Popup`. Tab can leave the popup and return; a close on the keyboard restores trigger focus, Base UI's own behavior. The two-part handle is exposed unchanged: `createPopoverHandle` as `Popover.createHandle` and `PopoverHandle` as `Popover.Handle`, the second v0.2 part pair on the namespace that mirrors Base UI's names, and the same question for Preview Card waits on [Hover Card's name, its contract, and whether hover-only disclosure is acceptable](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/111). Registry item `popover`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency.
- **Drawer.** Settled on [Drawer's parts, its styled split, and whether it varies the overlay recipe](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/113), over the facts in `docs/research/2026-09-16-base-ui-drawer.md`. Fifteen parts, six styled, no axes, no glyph. Tenth overlay and the third stated variation; its row, its split, the `Indent` decision, the Sheet boundary, and the edge are in The overlay set above, and the variation's two additions to the recipe are under Overlays. **Do not import `dialog`**: restate `Viewport`, `Backdrop`, `Popup`, `Title`, and `Description`, of which the last two are verbatim Dialog and the first two diverge, which is Combobox's shape rather than Alert Dialog's. `modal` stays Base UI's default and is **three-valued** — `true`, `false`, or `'trap-focus'`, which traps focus without locking page scroll or disabling outside pointer interaction — and `swipeDirection` is a pass-through defaulting to `down`. **Ultima writes `Popup`'s transform because there is nothing to override**: the part sets none of its own except transiently during a drag, and strips even that for a snap-point downward drawer. A vertical drawer using `snapPoints` reads `translateY(calc(var(--drawer-snap-point-offset) + var(--drawer-swipe-movement-y)))` rather than the movement variable alone. **Two variables are conditionally absent and both need a fallback**: `--drawer-height`, hence `var(--drawer-height, auto)`, and `--drawer-keyboard-inset`, which the provider sets only while the keyboard is aligned. **Nine CSS variables, all seeded as inline styles**, so no cascade layer can reach them, and five registered `inherits: false`, which means a rule on a descendant of the seeded element silently reads the initial value; every maintainer demo reads them on the element that seeds them. **Item 8 of the proof bar is heavier here than anywhere**: `Popup`'s resting transform contains the movement variable and its `height` carries the `auto` fallback, `Backdrop`'s opacity reads the progress variable, `Viewport` is `position: fixed`, and `SwipeArea` has a non-zero box. **Two behaviors sit outside the bar and the contract says so rather than leaving them silently uncovered**: the Android back gesture is Android and Chromium only and asks nothing of Ultima, and the swipe is a touch gesture on a document-level listener that a desktop Chromium run cannot reach. Asserting the CSS whose absence breaks the gesture is the coverage, which is what item 8 exists for. **Item 7 is not this component.** Registry item `drawer`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, and one `optimizeDeps.include` entry.
- **Context Menu.** Settled on [Context Menu against Dropdown Menu: restate the styled split, or compose it](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/109). Nineteen parts, eleven styled, no axes, three glyphs. Its overlay row, its styled split, and the trigger's contract are in The overlay set above. **`context-menu.tsx` restates Dropdown Menu's eleven style keys verbatim**, sixty-nine lines, and imports nothing from Ultima: `positioner`, `popup`, `item`, `selectionItem`, `linkItem`, `submenuTrigger`, `indicator`, `chevron`, `separator`, `groupLabel`, `arrow`. Nothing in that table is trigger-derived, so nothing legitimately diverges, and `popup`'s `transform-origin: var(--transform-origin)` is live here rather than inert as it is on `Dialog.Popup`, because Context Menu has a Positioner that seeds it. **Do not import `dropdown-menu`**, the same call [#107](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/107) already made for `menubar.tsx` one contract earlier on the same map. This is the second restatement in the system whose arrangement is meant to be identical rather than merely similar, after Alert Dialog against Dialog, and The registry item below records what that costs. Item 7 of the proof bar is not this component: the right click and the long press are the primitive's, which is item 4, and every style reacts to a `data-*` attribute, which is items 5 and 8; item 3 is the Menu clause, `data-highlighted` rather than a ring. Registry item `context-menu`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, and one `optimizeDeps.include` entry, which is the fifth of the five hand edits #107 priced.
- **Hover Card.** Settled on [Hover Card's name, its contract, and whether hover-only disclosure is acceptable](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/111). Eight parts, four styled, no axes, no glyph. Item `hover-card` over Base UI's `PreviewCard`, which is Naming's component-name clause; `hover-card.tsx` imports `{ PreviewCard } from '@base-ui/react/preview-card'` and reads its parts off that namespace, the way `dropdown-menu.tsx` reads Menu's. Its row, the Tooltip boundary, and the paint are in The overlay set above; its name source and the hover-disclosure rule are under the Accessibility contract. `delay` and `closeDelay` are **`Trigger` props**, defaults 600 and 300, and Ultima sets neither. `Handle` and `createHandle` pass through unchanged and `Handle` is a class, not a part. **The docs page carries the one rule no other page does**: a real `href` a reader can follow with the card never opening, every fact in the card also present at that destination, Escape dismissing it, the pointer travelling from link into card, and one sentence saying it opens on hover and on keyboard focus and never on touch. A demo with `href="#"` or a non-anchor trigger demonstrates the failure rather than the pattern. It needs no docs-page section of its own, writing no breakpoint. **Item 7 of the proof bar is not this component**; item 2 asserts the deliberate absence, that the trigger is queryable as a link by its own text and the popup carries no role, and item 8 is `Viewport`'s `position: relative`. Types follow `dropdown-menu.tsx`'s pattern, which erases Base UI's `Payload` generic on `Root` and `Trigger`, so a typed handle payload is not reachable through Ultima's types; that is already true of Menu and Popover. Registry item `hover-card`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, and one `optimizeDeps.include` entry, `@base-ui/react/preview-card`, which keeps the primitive's name though the item does not.
- **Menubar.** Settled on [Menubar's shape, given Base UI ships it as a container rather than a namespace](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/110). **One function, not a namespace**, and one styled part, no axes, no glyph. Its row, the declined namespace, and the paint are in The overlay set above. `orientation` is a pass-through with a `horizontal` and `vertical` style table setting `flex-direction`, which is `separator.tsx`'s shape and not an axis; `modal`, `disabled`, and `loopFocus` pass through at Base UI's defaults, which for `modal` is `true`. **Do not import `dropdown-menu`**: the menus in a bar are the consumer's, so its page names that item as a companion install and this one declares no registry dependency, which is deliberately not shadcn's Base UI backend importing thirteen names from its own `dropdown-menu` for appearance. **`data-has-submenu-open` is reachable only as an attribute selector.** Base UI's `className` and `style` accept a state function, `PartProps` omits both for Ultima's `style` slot, and `MenubarDataAttributes` and `useMenubarContext` are not exported, so neither Ultima nor a consumer can read `hasSubmenuOpen` programmatically; a consumer styles `':is([data-has-submenu-open])'` through the slot, and Ultima styles nothing on it because nothing about the bar should change when a menu opens. **The accessible name is required by the types** as one of `'aria-label'` or `'aria-labelledby'`, the third enforced attribute in the system and the reasoning is under the Accessibility contract. **Menubar's page is the first to print a second install command**, and substitutability is why: a wrapper trigger's `render={<Button />}` permits the consumer's own element, so those examples degrade, while a bar's contents must be Ultima's Dropdown Menu or the menus have no paint. It needs no docs-page section of its own, because it writes no breakpoint, which is the reason Navigation Menu declined one and Sidebar has one. The canonical demo follows Base UI's: four menus labelled File, Edit, View, and Help, at least one submenu, each trigger a ghost `sm` Button. **Item 8 of the proof bar is the bar's box**, tight around its triggers so the modal backdrop's cutout does not span the viewport; **item 7 is not this component**, since Ultima wires no behavior here. Registry item `menubar`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, and one `optimizeDeps.include` entry, which `packages/ui/vitest.config.ts` lacks today.
- **Accordion.** Settled on [Accordion's contract: its parts, its styled split, its heading level, and how far it copies Collapsible](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/143), over the findings in `docs/research/2026-09-17-accordion-after-apg-change.md`. Five parts, no axes, no glyph slot. `Root` passes through; `Item`, `Header`, `Trigger`, and `Panel` are styled, and the row, the paint, and the reasoning are in The Toggle, Accordion, Avatar, and Scroll Area set above. **`Trigger` is styled where `Collapsible.Trigger` is a wrapper trigger**, because an accordion trigger is a full-measure row of the accordion rather than a slot for a control the consumer already has, and the APG permits nothing beside the button inside the heading; its page says not to pass a Button through `render`, the obligation every styled trigger carries, and a nested Sidebar group therefore stays Collapsible's — the one boundary this component's arrival states about another, settled with the measure rule on [#164](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/164). **`Panel` takes no padding**: an element animating to `height: 0` cannot clip its own padding under either box model, so the content padding belongs to a wrapper inside the panel, which is what Base UI's demos do. **The heading level is `render`**, `Card.Title`'s answer, and no wrapper fuses `Header` into `Trigger`, which is shadcn's shape and the move that pins the level. **`orientation` and `loopFocus` are omitted**, both `@deprecated` no-ops since v1.6.0 removed roving focus following the APG, which is the dead-prop rule [#142](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/142) set for Toggle's `value`. **Style on `data-panel-open`, `data-open`, `data-starting-style`, `data-ending-style`, and `data-disabled` only**: `data-orientation`, `data-hidden`, `data-closed`, and `data-index` leak onto parts the docs do not list, `data-value` on a trigger holds the root's open-value array rather than that item's value, and `aria-disabled` renders on every trigger including `"false"`. A disabled trigger keeps `tabindex="0"` and takes `aria-disabled`, since `useButton` gets `focusableWhenDisabled: true`, which is the opposite of a disabled Button. **`role="region"` and `aria-labelledby` are unconditional and Base UI gives no switch**, so the APG's advice to drop the role past about six panels is unreachable without `render` and the page says the default is the primitive's rather than Ultima's. `multiple`, `disabled`, the value props, `Item.onOpenChange`, and `hiddenUntilFound` / `keepMounted` on both `Root` and `Panel` pass through at Base UI's defaults, which Ultima sets at neither level exactly as Collapsible sets neither; the value is an array either way, `multiple={false}` empties it when the open item is pressed, `eventDetails.cancel()` refuses that, and an item with no `value` gets a generated id so an initially-open accordion needs explicit ones. `--accordion-panel-width` is seeded and unread; Ultima reads height only. **Item 7 is not this component**, there being no `onKeyDown` anywhere under `accordion/`; **item 8 is Collapsible's three assertions over `--accordion-panel-height`**, and the `hiddenGuard` transfers with its `className` concatenation intact, applied outside the `stylex.props` merge or a consumer's `display: grid` through the style slot takes the guard with it. **Do not import `collapsible`**: the behavior is `useCollapsiblePanel`'s below Ultima, so an import would carry appearance alone and put that item in this one's derived `dependencies`; `collapsible.tsx` is unedited. Registry item `accordion`, one file, one new Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, five hand edits.
- **Avatar.** Settled on [Avatar's contract: its parts, its size and shape, and what an image without a name means](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/144), over the findings in `docs/research/2026-09-17-avatar-primitive-and-prior-art.md`. Three parts, all styled, no axes, no glyph slot. Its row, the paint, and the reasoning are in The Toggle, Accordion, Avatar, and Scroll Area set above. **`Root` is the visible circle rather than a group**, so it is the first entry in that set with nothing passed through: `position: relative`, a centred `inline-flex` box at space step 9 on both axes with `flex-shrink: 0`, `overflow: hidden`, `--ult-radius-full`, a `--ult-color-surface-sunken` ground, and `user-select: none`, with no border, since a ring between overlapping avatars is the group's question. **No `size` and no shape axis**: `size` is defined as the three control heights and an avatar is not a control, the sentence that already refused Switch, Checkbox, Radio Group, and Field, and no prior-art system sizes one by control heights anyway; the box and the radius are each a single unconditional declaration, so both are the `style` slot, which is **Skeleton**'s answer a second time. The general rule that decides both is stated in the set section: an axis earns its place when the look it carries is more than one declaration, or carries conditions a caller overriding through `style` would have to restate. **The initials track the box, not a text step**: `Root` takes `container-type: inline-size` and `Fallback` takes `font-size: 40cqi`, the system's first container query and **Iconography**'s `1em` rule one step further out, so a resized avatar needs no second override. `cqi` is a unit in a value, so no `@container` at-rule is involved and StyleX passes it through like `var()`. **`Fallback` is a layout slot and Ultima ships no default in it**: no `name` prop and no `getInitials`, because that would be a prop the Base UI part does not have, which Accordion's `level` was declined on, and because Chakra's and Mantine's derivations disagree with each other and both break on a mononym. A person icon is the caller's, rendered into the slot. **`alt` is documented and not type-enforced and Ultima defaults it to nothing**, for the reasons under the accessibility contract; `Root` is never named, and a decorative avatar takes `aria-hidden` on `Root` rather than on `Image` alone, or the initials reappear whenever the image fails. **`delay` and `keepMounted` pass through at Base UI's defaults and Ultima sets neither**, `keepMounted` being the only mode in which `next/image` and `loading="lazy"` work at all, since the default path preloads a detached copy and so sees no `src` on a `render` element and defeats a lazy attribute it never sees. **Ultima ships the stacking CSS unconditionally** — `Image` and `Fallback` both `position: absolute; inset: 0`, `Fallback` first in DOM order — so flipping `keepMounted` cannot break the component, and it costs the default mode nothing because the `<img>` is not in the DOM until it has loaded. **The hide is `visibility: hidden` on `':is([data-loading], [data-error])'` and `Image` sets no `display` anywhere**: an element with no box never intersects the viewport, so `display: none` would make the lazy image load never fire, which is the second clause of **Styled parts** in `Collapsible.Panel`'s `hiddenGuard` shape rather than `NavigationMenu.List`'s. `data-loading` and `data-error` exist only under `keepMounted`; `data-starting-style` appears in both modes and `data-ending-style` only in the default one, and no part emits the loading status, since `Root` maps it to `() => null`. **Item 7 is not this component**, Ultima wiring no behaviour and `onLoadingStatusChange` being the primitive's; **item 8 is the hide**, asserting that a not-yet-loaded `keepMounted` image computes `display` other than `none` and `visibility: hidden`. Item 1 is the no-props default alone, there being no axes. Three demos and no live demonstration, which the site owes none of. Registry item `avatar`, one file, one new Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, five hand edits.
- **Scroll Area.** Settled on [Scroll Area's contract: its six parts, its scrollbar visibility, and the focus ring it copies from Table.Scroll](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/145), over the findings in `docs/research/2026-09-17-scroll-area-visibility-and-overflow.md`. Six parts, three styled, no axes, no glyph slot. `Viewport`, `Scrollbar`, and `Thumb` are styled; `Root`, `Content`, and `Corner` pass through. Its row, the paint, and the reasoning are in The Toggle, Accordion, Avatar, and Scroll Area set above. **Base UI writes more inline style here than in any other primitive in the catalogue, and an inline style beats a StyleX class**, so the contract is mostly a list of what not to write: not `overflow` on `Viewport`, not `height` or `width` on `Thumb`, and not the `Scrollbar`'s absolute placement, each of which Base UI already sets and each of which would compile, ship, and never apply. **`Thumb` is not a second-clause part despite reading `--scroll-area-thumb-height`**, because the primitive reads it for you, inline. **What Base UI leaves out is the `Scrollbar`'s cross-axis size**: an unstyled scrollbar is zero pixels across and its thumb invisible, which is the first clause at its strongest and not the second, since the measurement, the attributes, and the drag all keep working behind a bar nobody can see. **`Viewport` is the second-clause part**: `block-size: 100%` and `box-sizing: border-box`, without which the viewport sizes to its content, nothing overflows, `hiddenState` stays both-hidden, `Scrollbar` and `Corner` return `null`, and `tabIndex` stays `-1`. **The bounded box is the consumer's**, on `Root` through the `style` slot, the third component after Skeleton and Avatar to put its box there, and its page says an unbounded Root never scrolls. **One visibility mode and no `type` prop**: the bar shows whenever its axis overflows and emphasizes on `':is([data-hovering], [data-scrolling])'` as a colour change, never an opacity gate, because `data-hovering` is never set for a touch pointer and a bar at `opacity: 0; pointer-events: none` cannot be grabbed, so every fade mode Radix and shadcn ship hides the component's only pointer affordance from the users least able to do without it. The five lines that produce the fade are on the docs page as a `style` override. **The ring is `Table.Scroll`'s, on `':focus-visible'`**, which matters more here than there because Base UI's explicit `tabIndex` makes the viewport click-focusable. `overflowEdgeThreshold`, `keepMounted`, and `Scrollbar`'s `orientation` pass through at Base UI's defaults and Ultima sets none; two are traps the page names — **`overflowEdgeThreshold` gates only the four `data-overflow-*` attributes and does nothing to the fade recipe**, which reads the unthresholded variables, and **`keepMounted` does not reproduce Radix's `always`**, clamping the thumb to `MIN_THUMB_SIZE = 16` px on a non-overflowing axis. **`CSS.registerProperty` for the four overflow variables is page-global and permanent** in Blink and Gecko, so after the first scroll area mounts, `var()` fallbacks for those names stop firing anywhere on the page and a descendant reader must write `--scroll-area-overflow-*: inherit` on itself and every element up to the viewport; harmless in WebKit, where the registration is skipped. The CSP note is a one-line pointer at **Registry and install**, Select's and Slider's pattern and the third occurrence. **Two docs-site retrofits ride with its build ticket**, settled on [#147](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/147) and written out under **The inventory, sorted**: the uncaptioned MDX table wrapper and the six palette ramp grids, the first of which is Scroll Area's rather than `Table.Scroll`'s because an uncaptioned table has no name for a `role="region"` and this viewport is `role="presentation"`. The shell content column stays page layout and loses its hidden scrollbar. Neither adoption is a live demonstration. **Item 7 is not this component**, scrolling being native and the drag the primitive's; **item 8 is `Viewport`'s `block-size`**, asserted as `scrollHeight` exceeding `clientHeight` with `tabindex="0"` in a Root bounded shorter than its content, which is the first item-8 assertion in the system to read layout rather than a computed declaration and which **Environment**'s real Chromium can run. Registry item `scroll-area`, one file, one new Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency and specifically not `table`, five hand edits.
- **Toggle.** Settled on [Toggle's contract: one part or a namespace, its axes, and what names it](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/142), over the item-versus-recipe finding on [#141](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/141). **One function, not a namespace**, because `@base-ui/react/toggle` exports one component and no parts. Two axes, `variant` (`outline`, `ghost`, default `outline`) and `size` (`sm`, `md`, `lg`, default `md`), in two flat tables that layer, since neither sets the other's properties. No `tone`, no glyph slot. Its row, the axis reasoning, and the paint are in The Toggle, Accordion, Avatar, and Scroll Area set above. The base table and `sizes` restate Button's, deliberately identically, so a toggle beside a button matches its height and hovers the same way; the pressed state is the one place the file departs from both neighbours, taking `--ult-color-accent-subtle`, `--ult-color-accent-border`, and `--ult-color-accent-text` where Toggle Group's item takes neutral `--ult-color-surface-raised`, because a standalone control has no sibling to be read against. **The pressed value carries its own `':hover'` nested inside it**, never as a sibling, for the reason now under State styling. **`value` is omitted and the `<Value extends string>` generic is dropped**: measured against the installed `@base-ui/react` 1.8.0, a standalone `Toggle` passes `value` to `useBaseUiId` and reads it only inside `groupContext?.setGroupValue`, so it identifies the toggle to a group that is not there, and [#141](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/141)'s reason for this being an item rather than a recipe is that a type is where a dead prop gets said. `pressed`, `defaultPressed`, `onPressedChange`, `disabled`, `nativeButton`, and `render` pass through; `pressed` is live here and inert inside a group, which is the mirror of `value`. **Do not import or edit `toggle-group.tsx`**, and it does not import this: Toggle Group composes none of Toggle's behavior, that being `ToggleGroupContext`'s below Ultima, so **Restatement** allows no import, the two item tables are meant to differ, and an import either way would put one item in the other's derived registry `dependencies`. **The accessible name is its text content and nothing is type-enforced**, which is Button's row; an icon-only Toggle is Icon-only Button's documented rule with `aria-label`, its glyph as the only child, and a `style` override squaring `paddingInline`. **Item 7 is not this component and neither is item 8**: every interaction is the primitive's and the one part paints under the first clause of Styled parts. **Item 5 is where the nesting is proved**, by asserting the pressed control's computed background equals its own hovered value and differs from a resting one's, which names no colour; Toggle is the first test in the tree to assert it. Registry item `toggle`, one file, one Base UI subpath, no Ultima component dependency, and four hand edits rather than five, because `@base-ui/react/toggle` is already in both vitest `optimizeDeps` lists.
- **Calendar.** Settled on [Calendar and Date Picker: shape, engine, and the ADR 0002 question](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/281), over the spine on [#277](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/277). Eighteen parts, no axes, two glyph slots, the first catalogue item whose primitive is a prop-getter library rather than a component. `Root` runs `useMachine` on the Zag `date-picker` machine with `inline` pinned and provides the api through the file's private context; every part spreads its getter and merges `stylex.props` through Zag's `mergeProps`, so the primitive's inline `style` survives where a getter emits one — the Scroll Area rule that an inline style beats a StyleX class, met here from the primitive side rather than the Base UI side. Parts fix their elements because the getters' semantics demand them — the table parts are a real `<table>` for the `role="grid"` contract — so there is no `render` prop, and `style` still merges last. **`Root` is the visible panel rather than a group**, Avatar's answer a second time: surface, hairline, radius, and padding on the inline box. Styled: `Root`, `Label`, `ViewControl`, `PrevTrigger`, `NextTrigger`, `ViewTrigger`, `RangeText`, `Table`, `TableHeader`, `TableCell`, `TableCellTrigger`, `MonthSelect`, `YearSelect`. Passed through: `Content`, `View`, `TableHead`, `TableBody`, `TableRow`. **`TableCellTrigger` is the heart of the paint**: `--ult-radius-md`, `--ult-color-surface-hover` on `:hover`, the accent fill with accent-contrast text on `data-selected`, a marker on `data-today`, `--ult-color-text-subtle` on `data-outside-range`, the disabled treatment on `data-unavailable` and `data-disabled`, and `data-in-range` with `data-range-start` / `data-range-end` carrying the selected run for `selectionMode="range"`. The input-and-popup half of the anatomy is Date Picker's alone: no `Control`, `Input`, `Trigger`, `ClearTrigger`, `PresetTrigger`, `Positioner`, or `Portal`, and `open`, `positioning`, `closeOnSelect`, `name`, `required`, and `placeholder` are omitted as dead on an inline grid — the second clause run at item level, the mechanical reason this is two items rather than one with a mode prop. `selectionMode`, `numOfMonths`, `startOfWeek`, `fixedWeeks`, the view props, `locale`, `timeZone`, `format`, `parse`, `min`/`max`, `isDateUnavailable`, `outsideDaySelectable`, `disabled`, `readOnly`, `translations`, `dir`, and `ids` pass through; `value`/`defaultValue`/`onValueChange` are typed in `@internationalized/date`'s `DateValue[]`, which is why that package is a declared dependency. `PrevTrigger` and `NextTrigger` hold private chevron glyphs, decorative, replaced through `children`. **Item 7 is not this component and neither is item 8**: Ultima wires no behavior on top of the machine, and the primitive reads no Ultima CSS — the one trap is `Content`'s `hidden` attribute, which nothing here may set a `display` against. Item 5 asserts `data-selected`, `data-today`, and `data-outside-range` each drive their style. Registry item `calendar`, one file, dependencies `@zag-js/react`, `@zag-js/date-picker`, `@internationalized/date`, and `@stylexjs/stylex`, no Ultima component dependency, five hand edits with the `optimizeDeps.include` entry for the Zag packages as the fifth.
- **Date Picker.** Settled on [Calendar and Date Picker: shape, engine, and the ADR 0002 question](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/281), over the spine on [#277](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/277). Twenty-five names — the machine's full 24-part anatomy plus `Portal`, the `@zag-js/react` portal utility exposed as a part so the popup matches every other Ultima overlay's shape — and one axis, `size` on `Control` (`sm`, `md`, `lg`, default `md`), the visual box carrying `size` the way Combobox's `InputGroup` does. `Root` runs the machine and provides the api through context, Calendar's architecture a second time. Styled: `Label`, `Control`, `Input`, `Trigger`, `ClearTrigger`, `PresetTrigger`, `Content`, `ViewControl`, `PrevTrigger`, `NextTrigger`, `ViewTrigger`, `RangeText`, `Table`, `TableHeader`, `TableCell`, `TableCellTrigger`, `MonthSelect`, `YearSelect`. Passed through: `Root`, `Portal`, `Positioner`, `View`, `TableHead`, `TableBody`, `TableRow`. **`Control` is the field box**: `--ult-color-surface-sunken`, the hairline, `--ult-radius-md`, the `size` heights, and the `:focus-within` ring, since focus lands on `Input` and `:focus-visible` on the group never matches — Combobox's exact case. **`Content` takes the overlay surface plus `outline: none`**: the machine emits `role="application"`, `aria-roledescription="datepicker"`, a translated `aria-label`, and `tabIndex={-1}`, and the `hidden` attribute it writes when closed is the item-8 trap — no `display` declaration may fight it. `Trigger` ships the private calendar glyph as decorative children and is named by the machine's translated `aria-label`; `ClearTrigger` holds a private x; `PrevTrigger`/`NextTrigger` hold the chevrons. `MonthSelect`/`YearSelect` keep native appearance: a compact Input-family paint and no glyph. **`date-picker.tsx` restates `calendar.tsx`'s grid paint rather than importing it**, the third pair meant to be identical rather than merely similar, after `dialog.tsx`/`alert-dialog.tsx` and `context-menu.tsx`/`dropdown-menu.tsx` — and like those two it is unchecked, the Restatement trade the system takes knowingly. It cannot compose: the picker's grid belongs to the picker's own machine, so there is no `Calendar` inside `Content` to depend on. `inline` is omitted — that is what `calendar` is. `open`/`defaultOpen`/`onOpenChange`, `positioning`, `closeOnSelect`, `required`, `name`, `placeholder`, and the `selectionMode` range pair's second `Input` at `index` 1 pass through; a `Field.Label` does not reach the machine's input, so the page documents `DatePicker.Label` or `aria-labelledby` as the association. Item 7 is not this component, item 8 is `Content`'s `hidden`, and item 3 covers the `:focus-within` ring on `Control`. Registry item `date-picker`, one file, the same four dependencies as `calendar`, no Ultima component dependency, five hand edits unless the Zag `optimizeDeps.include` already landed.

- **Button Group.** Settled on [The input-group set contracts: Button Group, Input Group, Input OTP, and Native Select](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/278). Two parts, `Root` and `Item`, three axes carried differently than anywhere else in the system. `Root` is a `<div role="group">` through `useRender`, an `inline-flex` row whose `flex-direction` follows `orientation` (`horizontal` default, `vertical`), a positional prop with a per-orientation style table, which is `separator.tsx`'s and Menubar's shape and not an axis. `Item` is a Base UI `Button` wearing Button's paint restated — the base table, the nested `variants[variant][tone]`, and `sizes` — plus a `joined` table keyed on `orientation` that does the work `button.tsx` cannot do for it: StyleX writes no combinators, so a wrapper's `style` cannot reach sibling radii, and each `Item` styles itself by position. `:not(:first-child)` zeroes the two corners on the touching side through the logical-radius longhands and drops the leading border (`border-inline-start-width: 0` horizontal, `border-block-start-width` vertical), so the seam is the previous item's end border — no negative margin and no z-index, both of which a token-free joined arrangement cannot pay for, and the focus ring survives untouched because it is an `outline`. `variant`, `tone`, and `size` sit on `Root` and reach `Item` through file-private context, Meter's `tone` shape generalised to three values, with `Item` accepting its own `variant` and `tone`, which win per the prop-wins clause of Cross-part state — a split-button group is the case. `size` is not overridable per item, because a mismatched height is the one difference that breaks the join. Defaults are Button's (`solid`, `md`, `accent`), so the restatement keeps the whole column. Keyboard is native: every item is a tab stop, there is no roving tabindex, and nothing here is `aria-pressed` — a set of controls that stay pressed is Toggle Group's, which is the boundary between the two items. `disabled`, `onClick`, and `render` pass through on `Item`. No `Text` or `Separator` part: a non-button cell between buttons is not this component's job, and the prior art's versions of both exist for shapes the catalogue answers elsewhere. `button.tsx` is not imported and not edited; the restatement is the registry item rule's appearance-not-behavior clause. Registry item `button-group`, one file, one Base UI subpath (`button`, already in both vitest `optimizeDeps` lists, so four hand edits), no Ultima component dependency. Item 7 of the proof bar is not this component.
- **Input Group.** Settled on [The input-group set contracts: Button Group, Input Group, Input OTP, and Native Select](https://github.com/frankieramirez/ultima/issues/278). Three parts, `Root`, `Input`, `Addon`; `size` on `Root` reaching the box, the three control heights, default `md`. `Root` is a plain `<div>` through `useRender` wearing the box `Combobox.InputGroup` already paints — restated, not imported, for the reason Combobox's own note gives, that `input` must not land in every install — sunken surface, strong border, `--ult-radius-md`, gap at space step 3, `width: 100%`, and the ring on `:focus-within`, the box lighting up while focus is inside it being the point of the component.
